906 entries, w-edwards-deming-operator to web3-specialist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **w-edwards-deming-operator**: Use this agent for operations review, quality systems, process variation analysis, and the question of whether a problem belongs to the worker or to the system the worker is in. Modeled on W. Edwards Deming — statistician, consultant, author of Out of the Crisis and The New Economics, and the man who taught postwar Japan how to measure variation, then waited thirty years for America to catch up. - **waiver-countersigner**: The pinned independent waiver countersigner (ADR-0001/ADR-0007). Dispatch this agent when a queued waiver was NOT mechanically eligible (countersign.sh process left it pending) — it answers exactly one question, "is this change actually waiver-eligible?", against the real diff, and writes countersign.json into the evidence bundle. Any-tier session can dispatch it; the model pin below is what makes the signature independent. Process refs ONE at a time (sequential), never in a batch prompt. - **warvis-maker**: WARVIS stage 3 — Mid-level TDD orchestrator. MEDIUM/LOW risk auto-proceeds. Only blocks on HIGH/CRITICAL. ([WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil](https://github.com/WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/warvis-maker.md))
- **warvis-orchestrator**: DEPRECATED — UoW lifecycle orchestration is now driven by the `/forge` skill in the main Claude Code session. This file is retained only as a redirect for callers using the legacy invocation. ([WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil](https://github.com/WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/warvis-orchestrator.md))
- **warvis-planner**: WARVIS stage 2 — Generate implementation plan from UoW spec. Calls devos_plan_dev_session. Writes .omc/plans/&lt;uow_id&gt;.md artifact. Reads project CLAUDE.md for commands. No approval gate except scope expansion beyond UoW boundaries. Works without Obsidian vault. ([WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil](https://github.com/WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/warvis-planner.md))
- **warvis-setup**: WARVIS project onboarding agent. Run once per project before warvis-orchestrator. ([WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil](https://github.com/WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/warvis-setup.md))
- **warvis-verifier**: WARVIS stage 4 — Run quality gates (Lint → Test → Security → Integration). Auto-detects tech stack and reads CLAUDE.md for commands. Calls devos_verify_dev_session. ([WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil](https://github.com/WooYoungSang/warvis-findEvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/warvis-verifier.md))
